[1] Why Do VLMs Fail at Remote Physiological Measurement? Rethinking with Physiological Grounding and Numerical Alignment cs.CVPDF

Xiao Yang, Zekun Zhang, Yuxuan Fan, Duoxun Tang, Ziyang Liu

TL;DR: VITAL 为 VLM 增加独立生理视觉通路，并进行结构化数值对齐，针对通用语义特征丢失细粒度生理线索和离散 token 不适于连续数值估计的问题。

Details

Motivation: 远程生理测量可服务视频健康监测，但现有方法多为专用模型，不易融入 VLM 的多模态交互；通用视觉语义表示可能弱化细微生理变化，自回归离散 token 预测也与连续心率数值不匹配。

Result: VITAL 保留原视觉通路作语义理解，并独立建立生理通路联合建模两类信息；结构化数值对齐进一步强化生成心率估计中的数值关系。作者在多个 rPPG 数据集上报告跨域估计提升，同时保留 VLM 通用视觉理解能力。摘要未给出数据集名称、指标或准确率增益。

Insight: 语义理解和生理信号提取可以共享整体模型但使用独立表示通路；数值关系建模也需不同于普通 token 生成。摘要定性报告跨域增益，无法判断对真实环境光照、运动干扰及硬件变化的适应程度。

Abstract: Video-based remote physiological measurement (RPM) has broad applications, while Vision-Language Models (VLMs) provide a promising opportunity to enable end-to-end physiological monitoring for future human-aware intelligent systems. However, existing RPM methods rely on task-specific models and cannot be seamlessly integrated with the multimodal understanding and interaction capabilities of VLMs. We revisit RPM with VLMs and provide empirical evidence for two major bottlenecks: generic visual semantic representations fail to preserve fine-grained physiological cues; while discrete token prediction in autoregressive generation is inherently misaligned with continuous numerical measurement. To address these challenges, we propose VITAL (Visual-physiological Token Alignment for Language Models), a visual-physiological token alignment framework for RPM and preserving general vision tasks. VITAL retains the original visual pathway for semantic understanding and introduces an independent physiological pathway to jointly model semantic and physiological information. Moreover, we propose a structured numerical alignment approach to improve the reliability of generative heart rate estimation by enhancing numerical relationship modeling. Extensive experiments show that VITAL improves cross-domain estimation performance on multiple rPPG datasets while preserving the general visual understanding capability of VLMs, demonstrating its effectiveness as a unified visual-physiological model. Code will be released.

[2] Action Jets: Learning the Local Shape of the Action-conditioned Data Manifold cs.CVPDF

Anton Arapin, Chahyon Ku, Bernadette Bucher

TL;DR: Action Jets 学习固定状态下动作条件图像流形的局部泰勒形状，让规划器一次提取后即可快速评估任意数量的短时域候选动作。

Details

Motivation: 动作条件世界模型能用于规划，但高维预测代价大，限制可评估动作数量；短时规划其实只需知道当前状态附近动作变化对未来图像的局部影响。

Result: 网络根据生成器采样监督，估计图像各区域沿每个动作轴单位变化的运动；校正头再用轨迹数据拟合至三阶泰勒多项式，并闭式计算有效半径。动作 Jet 的切线来自源生成器，其余高阶项从数据学习。一次状态推理后可遍历流形近似评估多个候选；单次提取评分 16 个候选比任一源生成器快 300 倍以上，候选数越多优势越大。在 BridgeData V2 的 7-DoF 操作、DL3DV 场景等评测中，短时预测比提供切线的 IRASim 更准，计算量低 100 倍以上；还比较了 WorldGym、FrameCrafter、DepthSplat、LagerNVS 等方法。

Insight: 若规划仅需局部动作响应，可学习局部流形近似避免每个候选都完整运行昂贵生成器。收益依赖动作范围仍处于多项式近似有效半径内；摘要未量化较长时域、强非线性动作下的误差增长。

Abstract: World models are a powerful means of reasoning and planning, but many of these action-conditioned predictive models are high dimensional and computationally expensive. These challenges limit the number of action candidates that can be evaluated by a planner. We show that this cost is avoidable for planners over short horizons, which only require local information with respect to the current state. For a fixed state, the frames reachable by varying a low-dimensional action (a camera movement or an end-effector command) lie on a low-dimensional manifold. We estimate the local shape of that manifold directly via a network trained to estimate how each image region moves per unit of each action axis supervised by data collected from samples by the generator. A correction head fit to trajectory data estimates the shape of this manifold region up to a third-order Taylor polynomial. We call this network estimating the Taylor polynomial describing the manifold shape an Action Jet and compute its radius of validity in closed form. By traversing this approximation of the local manifold shape, we only have to perform inference once per state for any number of candidate actions. Scoring sixteen candidates from a single extraction is over 300x faster than with either source generator, and this gap widens with the number of candidates a planner evaluates. We measure against state-of-the-art action-conditioned generators, IRASim and WorldGym on BridgeData V2, a real 7-DoF manipulation benchmark, and FrameCrafter on DL3DV scenes, where we chart from the novel-view generators DepthSplat and LagerNVS. Because only the tangent comes from the generator and every other term from the data, the Action Jet also predicts held-out frames more accurately than IRASim, the generator that supplied its tangent, at short horizons and at over 100x less compute.

[3] WorldMemBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Memory of Video World Models cs.CVPDF

Nan Zhou, Jianxiong Gao, Qiuyu Wang, Wen Wang, Jiahao Wang

TL;DR: WorldMemBench 评测视频世界模型的静态内容保持和动态事件演化记忆，并通过离开—重访轨迹检查重复返回后记忆是否衰减。

Details

Motivation: 交互视频模型需要既保留之前观察到的身份、物体及环境，又保证世界状态随时间合理演化；视觉保真和生成时长无法充分衡量历史记忆能力。

Result: 基准含 260 个测试案例，静态记忆检查人物身份/外观、物体外观/几何和环境内容/几何，动态记忆检查事件进展和状态持续性。通过受控相机离开与多次重访，静态内容与初始参考比对，动态事件与预期状态转移评估；外观、几何和事件指标组合，并补充视频质量与相机跟随诊断。九个代表模型的评测显示，静态保持强并不代表动态状态演化可靠；多次重访后记忆会进一步退化。人工研究支持自动评估与人类判断的一致性。

Insight: “记得场景外观”和“记得事件如何发展”是不同能力，多次返回会暴露首轮重访测不到的衰减。样本量为 260，摘要未列模型分数、人评规模或重访次数效应曲线。

Abstract: As video models enable longer and more realistic interactive experiences, maintaining a world consistent with its history becomes essential. This requires both preserving previously observed content and ensuring coherent world evolution over time. World memory is a critical capability to evaluate alongside visual fidelity and video duration. We introduce WorldMemBench, a benchmark for systematically evaluating world memory in video world models. Across 260 test cases, it assesses static memory through the retention of human identity and appearance, object appearance and geometry, and environmental content and geometry, and dynamic memory through event progression and state persistence. Using controlled camera departures and multi-revisit trajectories, the benchmark evaluates static content against fixed initial references and dynamic events against expected state transitions. It combines appearance and geometry metrics with event-specific assessments, and reports video quality and camera-following accuracy as complementary diagnostics. Evaluation of nine representative models shows that strong static retention does not imply reliable dynamic state evolution, while repeated revisits reveal memory degradation beyond the first return. Human studies support the alignment between our automatic evaluations and human judgments. WorldMemBench provides a systematic testbed for diagnosing memory failures and guiding the development of persistent, temporally coherent video worlds.

[4] MonoMIP: Multi-Height Inverse Perspective Mapping for Monocular 3D Object Detection cs.CVPDF

Emir Ulurak, Furkan Kıraç

TL;DR: MonoMIP 将多高度逆透视映射构造的度量鸟瞰特征与深度引导透视解码器结合，减轻单目 3D 检测中透视压缩造成的空间几何偏差。

Details

Motivation: 许多单目 3D 检测器依赖深度引导 Transformer，但仍完全在透视图像平面处理特征，受透视缩短影响而难表达空间关系。

Result: 方法将地面到屋顶之间 K 个高度平面做 IPM，形成有序高度条件度量 BEV 特征，减少离地物体误投影并暴露不同高度的外观变化。BEV 解码器用多尺度可变形注意力聚合每个查询的多高度信息，另有深度引导 3D 解码器从透视空间补充预测。在无额外训练数据条件下，KITTI val 的 Car Moderate AP3D|R40 为 25.84%，官方 test 为 21.66%，作者报告均超过近期单目方法；Waymo 上也报告优于比较方法，但摘要未给分数。

Insight: 单一地面平面 IPM 不足以表达离地物体，多高度特征可补足高度相关几何；双解码器保留透视与 BEV 互补。结果限于 KITTI/Waymo 协议，摘要未提供运行成本或各高度平面的消融。

Abstract: Monocular 3D object detection methods increasingly rely on depth-guided transformers, but they operate entirely in the perspective image plane where spatial relationships are distorted by foreshortening. We propose MonoMIP, a dual-decoder framework that adds complementary 3D geometric reasoning through Bird’s-Eye View (BEV) features constructed via Multi-Height Inverse Perspective Mapping. We perform IPM at K elevation planes spanning ground to rooftop, producing an ordered stack of height-conditioned metric BEV features that reduces above-ground misprojection and exposes depth-dependent appearance variation across planes. A BEV decoder with multi-scale deformable attention aggregates this multi-height evidence per query, while a depth-guided 3D decoder provides complementary perspective-space predictions. Without extra training data, MonoMIP attains 25.84% Moderate AP3D|R40 on KITTI val and 21.66% on the official test set for Car, outperforming recent published monocular methods on both the KITTI and Waymo benchmarks.

[5] The Gap Is in the Geometry: A Convergent Meta-Analysis of Multi-Facet Aerial and Remote Sensing Video Understanding cs.CVPDF

Georgios Angelidis, Aggelos Psiris, Maria Vakalopoulou, Georgios Th. Papadopoulos, Giorgos Sfikas

TL;DR: 该元分析认为航拍/遥感视频评测应关注几何条件变化、跨视角关系和闭环行动，而不只是抽帧多选题准确率，并提出 AVU-Card 报告卡。

Details

Motivation: 航拍视频研究常像网页视频一样用抽帧多选题测 MLLM 并报告准确率，但该协议可能测错能力，且难以反映高度、视角、物体尺寸和时间跨度等采集条件。

Result: 作者汇总已发表结果并分成四级证据，只有证据一致才形成结论。航拍基准上模型规模每增加十倍，准确率中位数仅提高 3.4 点，低于至少 19 点的人类差距；19 个论文内对照的 318 次评估中，315 次在采集条件变难时准确率下降。语料里没有航拍基准同时提供视频输入及逐任务人类/随机基线；闭环导航中能力从答题到行动明显坍塌。同一 MLLM 在航拍基准得分高于地面空间基准，但在跨视角几何研究中表现更低。摘要据此提出五条可证伪原则和 AVU-Card；末尾内容在当前摘要中截断。

Insight: 航拍感知高度依赖采集几何条件，单纯扩大模型或报告总体选择题分数可能掩盖几何推理与实际行动短板。结论为文献元分析，318 次对照和跨基准比较受已发表研究及任务协议限制；摘要末尾未完整提供建议细节。

Abstract: Aerial and remote sensing video is increasingly evaluated like web video, by posing multiple-choice questions to multimodal large language models (MLLMs) over sampled frames and reporting accuracy. This paper argues that this recipe measures the wrong aspect, and supports the claim with a convergent meta-analysis of a broad set of published results, organized in four evidence tiers, in which a claim becomes a position only when collected evidence agrees. Parameter scale exhibits a median of +3.4 accuracy points per tenfold increase on aerial benchmarks, far below a human gap of at least 19 points, whereas acquisition conditions control perception, as 315 of 318 evaluations in 19 within-paper contrasts lose accuracy when conditions such as altitude, view, object size or time horizon become harder. No aerial benchmark in the corpus combines video input with per-task human and random baselines, competence collapses from answering to acting in closed-loop navigation, and the same MLLMs score higher on the aerial benchmark than on ground spatial ones, but lower on studies that test cross-view geometry. From these findings the current study derives five falsifiable principles and proposes the AVU-Card, a reporting card for aerial video benchmarks

[6] Towards a Data-Centric Understanding of Spatial Reasoning in Multimodal Large Language Models cs.CV | cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Mahdieh Mallahnezhad, Masoud Jafaripour, Mohamadali Tofigh, Sheila Schoepp, Osmar R. Zaiane

TL;DR: 该综述从数据角度梳理 MLLM 空间推理微调，比较空间标注构造、模态/注释形式与评测，并归纳规则模板、LLM 提示、程序生成及人工参与四种数据构建路线。

Details

Motivation: MLLM 已用于具身智能和机器人视觉，但空间感知/推理仍有限；后训练被认为能改善能力，而相关数据如何构造、标注和评估缺少系统综述。

Result: 作者综述从监督微调到强化学习的预训练 MLLM 微调方法，重点分析数据集设计、构建及模型评估。总结四类固定空间标注构造方式：规则与模板、LLM 提示、程序化生成、人类在环；同时指出不同研究采用的评分、容差指标和基准差异很大。未来方向包括统一动态标注、更高效空间推理、标准化评测、扩大空间范围和以人为中心的空间交互。摘要未报告新算法或量化基准结果。

Insight: 空间推理能力评测和训练结果高度依赖数据定义及容差协议，数据构造范式需要与动态任务相匹配；统一评测标准可改善横向比较。本文为综述，归纳范围受纳入文献和空间任务分类方式限制。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have been widely applied in embodied agents and robotics for vision tasks. However, MLLMs suffer from limitations in spatial reasoning and perception. Post-training techniques have shown promise for improving these capabilities; yet, there are limited data-driven surveys of such approaches. In this survey, we consider approaches to fine-tuning pretrained MLLMs for spatial reasoning tasks, ranging from supervised to reinforcement learning, with an emphasis on the datasets used. We investigate themes of dataset construction, data modality and annotation, and evaluation of the resulting models. Based on these themes, we categorize research into three groups: dataset design, dataset construction and evaluation. We find four main methods for constructing fixed spatial annotations: rules and templates, LLM prompting, procedural generation, and human-in-the-loop. We also observe substantial variation in evaluation approaches, including different scoring methods, tolerance metrics, and benchmark types. Finally, we identify opportunities for future research in unified dynamic annotation, efficient spatial reasoning, standardization of evaluation, expanded spatial range, and human-centered spatial interactions.

[7] A Gradient-Regularized Deep Learning Method for Restoring Image in Myopic Vision cs.CVPDF

Kebin Contreras, Luis Toscano-Palomino, Rafael Torres, Jorge Bacca

TL;DR: 该研究用带空间梯度正则的 U-Net 生成适配屈光模糊的变换图像，旨在让固定距离的近视者更清晰感知场景细节。

Details

Motivation: 近视的球面离焦会削弱人眼对细空间细节的感知，研究目标是对输入图像做计算变换以补偿指定近视条件下的视觉退化。

Result: 方法用二阶 Zernike 多项式 Z₂⁰ 建模离焦，在 KITTI 数据上训练 U-Net，并用重建误差与空间差分结构保持的 L1 正则联合优化；测试在 DIV2K 和 ImageNet 上进行。基线 PSNR 随数据集及严重程度为 19.47–27.42 dB，梯度正则方法提升 1.48–7.34 dB，并报告感知质量优于经典基线。摘要未说明受试者视觉实验或主观清晰度评估。

Insight: 训练中强调梯度/边缘结构有助于补偿离焦导致的高频细节模糊，并表现出跨图像分布泛化。该结果基于仿真 Zernike 模糊和图像指标，不等于临床视力改善或对真实个体眼球像差的验证。

Abstract: Myopic vision degrades the quality of human vision due to spherical eye defocus aberrations that impair the perception of fine spatial details. To address this limitation, this work proposes a deep learning-based approach to computationally design transformed images that allow individuals with myopia in fixed distance to perceive scenes with enhanced clarity. The method trains a neural network to counteract defocus aberrations modeled using the second-order Zernike polynomial $Z_2^0$. The approach employs a U-Net architecture trained with a loss function combining reconstruction error minimization with spatial difference structural preservation through $\ell_1$ regularization. Training is carried out exclusively on the KITTI dataset, while testing is performed on DIV2K and ImageNet datasets, demonstrating generalization across diverse image distributions. Under myopic conditions modeled by $Z_2^0$, baseline performance shows visual degradation with PSNR values ranging from 19.47 to 27.42 dB across datasets and severity levels. The proposed gradient-regularized method achieves consistent improvements, delivering PSNR gains ranging from 1.48 to 7.34 dB and significantly lower perceptual compared to classical baselines.

[8] Long-horizon Video Generation with Slot Memory Addressed By Region Tokens cs.CVPDF

Eren Cetin, Julius Erbach, Vukasin Bozic, Hyojun Go, Zixiang Zhao

TL;DR: SMART 以固定容量、由相机区域 token 寻址的 slot 记忆存储视频内容，并根据当前渲染视角读取历史，使重访召回取决于视角几何而非记忆新旧。

Details

Motivation: 长视频生成记忆需要容量有界以支持无限续写，同时能在相机返回某位置时恢复很久以前观察的内容。显式几何缓存存储会增长，循环记忆则可能随时间淡忘旧观察。

Result: SMART 将内容写入固定大小的 slot 循环记忆，写入由观察相机寻址，读取由渲染相机查询，不构建显式 3D 表示也不扩展缓存；读取还给出置信度，供分块因果扩散 Transformer 判断信任召回内容或依赖生成先验。在 Memory Maze 和 Minecraft 长轨迹上，作者报告模型在远超训练时域的条件下仍能高保真召回，并维持常量记忆和每步成本。摘要未给具体记忆指标或模型对比数值。

Insight: 以区域/视角寻址而非时间近邻寻址，旨在同时获得固定容量和空间重访记忆；置信度也可控制历史证据的使用。摘要未说明长期 slot 冲突、场景规模上限或不同重访间隔的具体退化曲线。

Abstract: Memory for long-horizon video generation must satisfy two competing requirements: it must remain bounded so generation can continue indefinitely, while still recovering what was observed at a place when the camera returns, regardless of how long ago it was seen. Reconstruction-based memories provide spatial recall by caching and reprojecting explicit geometry, but require growing storage and depend on estimated 3D structure. Recurrent memories keep a constant-size state, but retention is governed by temporal recurrence, causing old observations to fade with time. We introduce SMART, a slot memory addressed by region tokens, which combines bounded storage with spatially addressed recall. SMART stores content in a constant-size slot recurrence whose writes are addressed by the observing camera and whose reads are queried by the rendering camera, without constructing an explicit 3D representation or maintaining a growing cache. Consequently, recall is conditioned on inter-view geometry rather than on recency. Each read additionally provides a confidence estimate that conditions a chunk-causal diffusion transformer, allowing it to selectively trust retrieved content while relying on its generative prior elsewhere. On long trajectories in Memory Maze and Minecraft, SMART maintains high-fidelity recall far beyond its training horizon while using constant memory and per-step cost.

[9] LAMP: 3D Source-Aware Lighting Estimation at Multiple Points cs.CVPDF

Yanqiao Wang, Jin Sun

TL;DR: LAMP 从单张 RGB 图像和标记的查询点估计各光源对局部照明的贡献及其 3D 方向，并用轻量辐照度网络辅助重建稠密光照。

Details

Motivation: 传统光照估计恢复整体照明分布，却通常不显式说明某个场景位置的光照来自哪些物理光源。

Result: LAMP 构建共享有效光源清单，为每个查询点排序贡献光源并预测查询点到光源的三维方向。监督来自程序化控制室内场景，复用独立渲染的光源响应作为不同照明配置下贡献标签，且由光源/查询点位置获得精确方向。在 Infinigen 测试集上，微调 Qwen3-VL-8B 后，主导光源 3D PCK@0.5m 从 0% 提升至 70.6%；在正确定位的来源中，94.5% 方向与真值夹角小于 12.5°。结合轻量 RGB 辐照度网络后，探针渲染质量与既有光照估计方法相当，同时还能定位并归因单个物理光源。

Insight: 从整体辐照度恢复推进到来源级解释，需要将语义光源识别与几何方向联合监督；程序化独立光源渲染提供了来源贡献真值。精度在 Infinigen 测试集报告，真实复杂场景的光源遮挡和材质误差影响尚不清楚。

Abstract: Existing illumination estimation methods primarily recover lighting distributions for rendering, but typically leave the contributions of individual physical light sources at specific scene locations implicit. We address this gap by leveraging the scene understanding capabilities of vision-language models (VLMs) to connect local illumination with its physical sources. We introduce LAMP, a framework for point-conditioned, source-aware illumination reasoning from a single RGB image with marked query points. LAMP constructs a shared inventory of active light sources, ranks contributing sources for each query, and predicts their 3D query-to-source directions. We supervise these relationships using procedurally controlled indoor scenes, reusing independently rendered source responses to derive contribution targets across lighting configurations and obtaining exact directions from source and query positions. On the Infinigen test set, fine-tuning Qwen3-VL-8B increases dominant-source 3D PCK@0.5m from 0% to 70.6%, with 94.5% of directions within 12.5 degrees of ground truth among correctly localized sources. Combined with a lightweight network predicting dense RGB irradiance, LAMP achieves probe-rendering quality comparable to prior lighting-estimation methods while additionally localizing individual physical sources in 3D and explicitly attributing local illumination to them.

[10] Kandinsky S2V 1.0: Audio-Driven Avatar Video Generation cs.CVPDF

Nikolay Semenov, Artem Riabinin, Sergey Bassenko, Anna Averchenkova, Julia Agafonova

TL;DR: Kandinsky S2V 提供 2B Lite 和 21B Pro 语音驱动人像视频模型，从单张参考图、语音和文本生成长视频并同步嘴型、表情、姿态和手势。

Details

Motivation: 语音到视频头像生成需在长时生成中协调唇动、表情、姿势与手势，并支持多样化语音条件和人物参考。

Result: 模型使用 Qwen2.5-Omni 提供文本和音频条件，采用参考感知三级视觉记忆；训练从 Kandinsky 5.0 Video 开始，结合混合参考记忆布局和区域自适应人脸损失。数据集含 117 万段通过主动说话人检测筛选的单说话人视频，英语和俄语描述直接生成。并排人工评估中 Pro 平均与最强开源及两种商业模型相当或更好，唇同步也更受偏好；Lite/Pro 在五项通用语音到视频指标平均排名靠前。π-Flow 蒸馏 Pro 以 16 步、无需 classifier-free guidance 生成，端到端比基础模型快 5.4 倍，且几乎所有报告基准指标持平或更高。

Insight: 大规模单说话人语料、参考视觉记忆和区域自适应损失支撑长时人像一致性；蒸馏进一步显著缩短采样。摘要未提供人工评估人数、各项同步/视觉绝对分数和不同语种差异。

Abstract: We present Kandinsky S2V 1.0, an open family of speech-to-video models for controllable audio-driven human animation, comprising Kandinsky S2V 1.0 Lite (2B parameters) and Kandinsky S2V 1.0 Pro (21B parameters). From a single reference image, a speech waveform, and a text prompt, the models generate long-form 25 FPS video in which lip motion, expression, pose, and gesture follow the speech. The architecture draws text and audio conditioning from one Qwen2.5-Omni model and adds a reference-aware three-scale visual memory. Training starts from the Kandinsky 5.0 Video foundation models [1] and combines mixed reference-memory layouts with an area-adaptive face loss. The training corpus contains 1.17M single-speaker clips selected by active-speaker detection, with captions generated directly in English and Russian. A side-by-side human evaluation against the strongest open-source baseline and two proprietary commercial models shows that Kandinsky S2V 1.0 Pro performs comparably to or better than these models on average and is also preferred for lip synchronization. On common speech to video benchmarks, Kandinsky S2V 1.0 Lite and Kandinsky S2V 1.0 Pro rank among the top performing models according to mean rank over five reported metrics. Beyond the base models, we provide π-Flow distilled variants of both Kandinsky S2V 1.0 Lite and Kandinsky S2V 1.0 Pro. The distilled Kandinsky S2V 1.0 Pro model generates video in 16 sampling steps without classifier-free guidance, achieving a 5.4 times end-to-end speedup over the base model, while matching or exceeding it on nearly all reported benchmark metrics. To accelerate open research and deployment in speech-to-video generation, we release the code, model checkpoints, and diffusers integration under the MIT license.

[11] SonoWorld: Towards Interactive Pose-Controllable Ultrasound World Models cs.CVPDF

Zefan Yang, Zheyuan Zhang, Boris Maihe, Yikang Liu, Hang Yu

TL;DR: SonoWorld 从未标注临床超声视频学习因果生成，再用少量电磁追踪自由手扫描将生成动态映射到公制 6-DoF 探头动作。

Details

Motivation: 机器人超声可标准化采集并降低操作负担，但缺少真实且可控的仿真环境；超声帧只是未观测 3D 解剖的截面，探头微小移动也可能造成画面大变，使自然图像世界模型难以适用。

Result: 三阶段训练先在约 690 万张多器官未标注超声帧上预训练潜动作条件视频扩散模型，再用少量电磁追踪甲状腺扫描将动态对齐公制探头姿态，最后用分布匹配蒸馏实现因果长程自回归生成，并选择性缓存/淘汰 token。作者以姿态跟随保真度、往返一致性、合成动作轴向控制及用户研究评测。在基于模型的动作判别中，SonoWorld 在最多 74.8% 试次中能正确判断目标方向探头动作优于等幅反方向动作。摘要称其主要由自由手临床视频学习，是首个因果、姿态可控超声世界模型。

Insight: 大量临床视频提供外观动态，少量有姿态标注扫描提供动作 grounding，二者分阶段结合缓解真实监督稀缺。74.8% 为模型评判动作排序的比例，摘要未列用户研究结果和长时生成指标，不能直接等同于机器人扫描成功率。

Abstract: Robotic ultrasound can standardize image acquisition and reduce operator workload and variability, but policy learning remains limited by the lack of realistic, controllable simulation environments. While controllable world models have advanced rapidly in natural-image domains, ultrasound world modeling remains challenging because each frame captures only a cross-section of unobserved 3D anatomy, causing small probe motions to induce large appearance changes. Existing approaches rely on robot-tracked poses and latent-space prediction, but do not directly generate the observations required for interactive control. We propose SonoWorld, a causal ultrasound world model that learns video dynamics from unlabeled clinical videos and grounds them to 6-DoF control using a small set of freehand clinical scans tracked with an EM sensor. SonoWorld is trained in three stages: pretraining a latent-action-conditioned video diffusion model on around 6.9 million unlabeled multi-organ ultrasound frames; grounding the learned video dynamics to metric probe poses with a small EM-tracked thyroid dataset; and applying distribution matching distillation to enable causal, long-horizon autoregressive generation with selectively caching eviction tokens at inference time. We evaluate controllability through pose-following fidelity, round-trip consistency, and axis-specific control under synthetic actions on held-out tracked scans, and further validate it with a user study. In model-based action discrimination, SonoWorld correctly ranks the target-directed probe motion over an equal-magnitude opposite motion in up to 74.8% of trials. To our knowledge, SonoWorld is the first causal, pose-controllable ultrasound world model learned primarily from freehand clinical videos, providing a data-driven interactive environment for robotic ultrasound data acquisition and policy evaluation.

[12] NavAble: A Large-Scale Dataset and Synthetic Data Generation Pipeline for Blind Navigation cs.CV | cs.ROPDF

Hochul Hwang, Jahir Sadik Monon, Soowan Yang, Shiven Umeshbhai Patel, Anh Nhat Hong Nguyen

TL;DR: NavAble 提供面向盲人/低视力导航的可及性关键物体数据，并结合真实图像和可控 3D 合成数据扩大类别、环境和视角覆盖。

Details

Motivation: 识别可听行人信号、门按钮、扶手等物体对无障碍导航重要，但现有大规模视觉数据对此类类别覆盖少、标注不一且多样性不足。

Result: NavAble 含 11 类，结合 4.1 万张筛选开放图像与 8,000 张新采集且密集标注的真实图像；合成流程从 565 个资产在 37 种环境、不同风格视角生成 452,000 帧及真值标注。自动流程结合网络图像收集、VLM 筛选、SAM 3D 资产生成和相机轨迹配置。现有分割模型在该数据微调后，留出集 mIoU 超过 80%；加入合成数据后，较仅真实数据训练最高提升 3.5 mIoU。两种移动辅助机器人第一人称数据验证也显示真实导航感知可靠性提升。

Insight: 数据集将真实分布与可控合成几何结合，针对长尾无障碍对象和具身视觉覆盖不足；合成数据确有最高 3.5 mIoU 增益。摘要未给两个机器人具体导航任务成功率或类别级误差，80% mIoU 也限于作者留出集。

Abstract: Reliable recognition of accessibility-critical objects (e.g., audible pedestrian signals, door-activation buttons, and handrails) is essential for assistive navigation technologies that support safe, independent mobility for blind and low-vision (BLV) users. Yet these categories are underrepresented in existing large-scale vision datasets, and the few datasets that include them suffer from limited class coverage, inconsistent annotations, and poor diversity. We introduce NavAble, a large-scale dataset spanning 11 accessibility-critical object classes, combining 41K curated open-source images with 8K newly collected, densely annotated real-world images. To extend scale and diversity, we develop a synthetic data generation pipeline that renders high-fidelity 3D assets across 37 environments under diverse styles and viewpoints, yielding 452K frames with rich ground-truth annotations from 565 assets. The pipeline minimizes manual effort by combining automated web image crawling, VLM-based filtering, SAM 3D-based asset generation, and convenient camera trajectory configuration, enabling large-scale data generation at a diversity unattainable through manual collection. Experiments show that fine-tuning existing segmentation models on the NavAble Dataset achieves reliable accessibility-object segmentation (>80% mIoU on our held-out test set). We also demonstrate the effectiveness of synthetic data augmentation, which yields up to +3.5 mIoU over real-world-only fine-tuning. Beyond benchmark evaluation, validation on egocentric data from two mobility-assistive robots demonstrates improved perception reliability in real-world navigation scenarios. Together, NavAble establishes a scalable foundation for BLV navigation perception, directly addressing the data scarcity and embodiment gaps. The dataset and pipeline are publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/NavAble/NeurIPS_2026_BLV.

[13] MMKG-R1: Constructing Multimodal Knowledge Graphs via Reinforcement Learning cs.CVPDF

Zongyu Wu, Yilong Wang, Xiaochen Wang, Minhua Lin, Yuan Zhong

TL;DR: MMKG-R1 以图谱质量为直接奖励训练多模态知识构建器，评价单个信息源抽取和最终组装图谱，而不只依赖下游问答正确性作为间接信号。

Details

Motivation: 多模态图 RAG 的效果取决于构建的知识图谱质量，但现有方法常缺少显式质量指导；只按最终答案正确性做 RL 难提供针对图谱本身的直接反馈。

Result: 作者提出多个质量感知奖励，分别评估从单个知识源抽取的信息质量和组装后的多模态知识图谱质量，并用这些奖励强化学习训练 MLLM 作为图谱构建器。在多个数据集上，作者报告生成的图谱改善下游问答准确率。摘要未列数据集名称、具体图谱质量指标或 QA 增益。

Insight: 将奖励作用于图谱抽取与结构组装本身，能提供比终局 QA 对错更直接的构造信号。摘要未说明奖励定义、人工图谱质量评估和对下游问答提升的量化幅度。

Abstract: Multimodal knowledge graphs (MMKGs) organize information across different modalities to support multimodal Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), which aims to improve the performance of multimodal large language models (MLLMs). The effectiveness of these multimodal Graph RAG methods largely depends on the quality of the constructed MMKGs. However, most existing methods construct MMKGs without explicit guidance on graph quality, while reinforcement learning (RL)-based methods that rely on final-answer correctness may provide only indirect feedback on MMKG quality. To address these limitations, we propose MMKG-R1, a novel MMKG-quality-driven RL framework for training an MLLM as a multimodal knowledge constructor. To be specific, MMKG-R1 introduces multiple quality-aware rewards to directly evaluate both the quality of information extracted from individual knowledge sources and the quality of the assembled MMKG. These rewards are then used to guide the RL training. Experiments across multiple datasets show that MMKG-R1 constructs MMKGs that improve downstream question-answering accuracy.

[14] VLX-Go: Robust Embodied Tracking through Multi-Stage Learning and On-Policy Refinement cs.CV | cs.CL | cs.ROPDF

Kyusong Lee, Heting Ying, Tiancheng Zhao

TL;DR: VLX-Go 以分阶段多模态训练学习机器人中心路点跟随策略，并通过策略访问状态的 oracle 标签进行在线精炼，重点改善有干扰对象时的目标跟踪。

Details

Motivation: 跟随单一人物相对简单，但在多人或干扰对象中持续跟随指定目标困难；现有方法从单目标转向干扰跟踪时成功率明显下降。

Result: 策略结合冻结视觉编码器、多尺度时间上下文、Transformer 和机器人中心路点预测头。训练依次进行视觉—语言对齐、多模态推理中训练和跟踪专门化，使用 19,532 条 oracle episode。完整多阶段配方使干扰跟踪成功率从此前 OmTrackVLA 的 41.54% 提至 55.80%；再用策略访问状态的筛选 oracle 标签进行 on-policy 精炼，额外提高 2.42 个百分点至 58.22%，碰撞率下降 1.99 点。最终方法在比较系统中干扰及歧义跟踪成功率最高。

Insight: 先对齐表征、再学多模态推理和跟踪动作，再从策略自身访问状态获取监督，能逐步减少干扰跟踪失败。on-policy 结果属初步研究，摘要未提供环境规模或不同跟踪距离下的表现。

Abstract: Embodied visual tracking requires a robot to identify a designated target from visual observations and control its own motion to follow that target through an environment. Following an isolated person is a comparatively simple demonstration of embodied visual tracking, but following the intended person among distractors remains challenging. Existing methods exhibit a substantial drop in success from single-target to distractor tracking, motivating a focus on the shared spaces that person-following robots must navigate. We present \method{}, a vision–language waypoint policy built around the hypothesis that visual and language representations should be aligned before tracking-specific motion is learned. The policy combines complementary frozen visual encoders, multiscale temporal context, a transformer backbone, and a lightweight head for robot-centric waypoint prediction. Its training progresses through vision–language alignment, multimodal reasoning mid-training, and tracking specialization on 19,532 oracle episodes. Compared with 41.54% distractor-tracking success for our earlier OmTrackVLA model, the complete multi-stage recipe achieves 55.80%. We then explore on-policy refinement using filtered oracle labels for states visited by the policy. This preliminary study adds 2.42 percentage points of distractor-tracking success, reaching 58.22%, while reducing collision rate by 1.99 pts. The final policy records the highest distractor- and ambiguous-tracking success rates among the compared methods. These results support staged multimodal learning as a promising direction for tracking among distractors and motivate further study of on-policy refinement for person following in shared spaces.

[15] The Impact of Detector Architecture, Model Scale, and Scene Density on Multi-Pedestrian Tracking cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Pedro Manoel Herminio Alves, Dalton Cézane Gomes Valadares, Cláudio de Souza Baptista

TL;DR: 该研究在 MOT17、MOT20、SOMPT22 上比较 YOLO11、RT-DETR、YOLO26 检测器与 ByteTrack、BoT-SORT 的组合，并分析模型规模、场景密度和推理方式对多行人跟踪的影响。

Details

Motivation: 拥挤场景中的遮挡和快速运动使行人跟踪更困难，检测器架构、规模、场景密度及 NMS/端到端推理选择都会影响最终轨迹质量和速度。

Result: RT-DETR 系列在三个基准及两种跟踪器下平均 HOTA 最高，在拥挤的 MOT20 上优势更明显，但计算成本较高。MOT17 和 SOMPT22 上，YOLO26 变体（尤其带 NMS 的 YOLO26-L）提供较好的质量—速度折中。YOLO26 对比中，端到端推理未能稳定优于 NMS 方案。摘要未提供具体 HOTA、时延或硬件数据。

Insight: 检测器选择应结合场景密度和速度约束：最高跟踪质量与最佳效率未必来自同一模型；去除 NMS 也不保证持续改善。具体推荐需根据部署算力和任务优先级，而非只看单一基准平均成绩。

Abstract: Pedestrian tracking in video is important for applications such as surveillance and scene analysis, but it becomes more challenging in crowded scenarios with frequent occlusions and rapid motion. This paper analyzes how detector architecture, model scale, scene density, and inference regime affect the tracking of multiple pedestrians. We compare the YOLO11, RT-DETR, and YOLO26 detector families with the ByteTrack and BoT-SORT trackers on sequences from the MOT17, MOT20, and SOMPT22 benchmarks. For YOLO26, we also compare traditional overlap filtering via Non-Maximum Suppression (NMS) with end-to-end inference. The RT-DETR family achieved the highest mean scores on the HOTA tracking quality metric across all three benchmarks with both trackers, with a more pronounced advantage on MOT20, albeit at a higher computational cost. On MOT17 and SOMPT22, YOLO26 variants, especially YOLO26-L with NMS, offered favorable combinations of quality and speed compared with RT-DETR. In the YOLO26 inference comparison, the end-to-end mode did not yield consistent tracking gains over the NMS-based regime. Taken together, these results provide a practical guide for selecting detectors and trackers for this task based on application priorities.

[16] WinARDet: Compressed Window Auto-Regression for Long-Horizon Vision-Language Grounding cs.CVPDF

Chenxu Wang, Hangyi Kuang, Bo-Wen Yin, Yuxuan Li, Quan-Sheng Zeng

TL;DR: WinAR 将长坐标生成拆成连续窗口，并用固定数量潜 token 压缩历史窗口，减少坐标历史注意力对新视觉证据的压制。

Details

Motivation: 密集场景 MLLM 检测器自回归生成坐标时可能陷入重复框循环；作者将其归因于历史答案注意力接管，使重复坐标 token 挤占原本关注新图像证据的注意力。

Result: Compressed Window Auto-Regression 在当前窗口内保留完整 token 自回归，把此前各窗口压缩成固定潜 token 条件后续生成，兼顾减少原始坐标重复暴露与跨窗口多目标依赖。在每图含 200–2,000 个对象的 RSDense 上，WinARDet 将 RexOmni IoU=0.5 的 F1 从 32.16 提至 39.36，冗余率从 35.48% 降至 13.00%。作者还报告其可泛化至并行框解码和多边形输出格式。

Insight: 窗口级压缩缓解历史坐标累积造成的注意力偏置，同时保留一定跨窗条件信息，针对的是长答案密集检测的特有解码故障。摘要未给推理延迟和潜在 token 对定位精度的其他影响。

Abstract: MLLM-based detectors formulate vision-language grounding as autoregressive coordinate generation, yet in dense scenes, they often collapse into a box repetition loop, repeatedly emitting identical boxes until the decoding budget is exhausted. We trace this failure to historical-answer attention takeover: Under full-history autoregression, repeated coordinate tokens in the growing answer history attract disproportionate attention, crowding out the attention sinks needed to attend to new visual evidence. % To break this loop, in this paper, we propose \textbf{Compressed Window Auto-Regression} (WinAR). This new mechanism partitions a long answer sequence into consecutive generation windows and restricts full token-level autoregression to the current window only while compressing each previous window into a fixed set of latent tokens that condition subsequent decoding. This design reduces the repeated exposure to raw historical coordinates that triggers the attention takeover, yet preserves cross-window dependencies essential for coherent multi-object generation. On RSDense, which contains 200-2,000 objects per image, our model WinARDet improves RexOmni F1-score (IoU=0.5) from 32.16 to 39.36 over the baseline, while reducing the redundancy rate from 35.48% to 13.00%. WinARDet also generalizes across decoding strategies and output formats, including parallel box decoding and polygon generation, providing a practical paradigm for long-horizon vision-language grounding across scene densities.

[17] VideoHarness-SRSI: Structured Recursive Self-Improvement for Video Understanding Harness cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Zihao Lin, Haoyu Dong, Haobo Xu, Chi-Chih Chang, Xuying Ning

TL;DR: VideoHarness-SRSI 在冻结 VLM 外部优化视频理解 harness，通过独立探测分支、内容无关反馈和证据准入，缓解递归优化中的验证集过拟合与早期经验陷阱。

Details

Motivation: 系统能力不仅取决于模型权重，也取决于工具、信息获取、推理及记忆的可执行 harness；递归改进若反复复用有限且噪声大的反馈，可能只学会过拟合验证数据。

Result: 初始框架 VideoHarness-BRSI 演化 4,000 多个候选，虽可与手工系统竞争，却出现验证过拟合、搜索预算稀释和早期经验陷阱；作者将其归因于同一不完美验证证据同时指导 harness 修改和搜索策略。SRSI 将每个搜索决定限定在可迁移、充分且搜索承诺前收集的证据上：提议器只接收无内容反馈，探索由带仪器的预热实验指导，机制先在隔离 probe-race 分支发展，再做因子组合和联合细化。四个视频理解基准上，等权平均留出分比 BRSI 高 6.2%，验证—测试差距平均降低 37.9%。

Insight: harness 优化需要把“搜索如何选方向”与“候选如何按验证分数采纳”分开，避免单一验证集信号同时驱动两者形成闭环过拟合。提升来自四基准留出成绩；摘要未列单基准结果及 4,000 候选的具体计算预算。

Abstract: LLM system capabilities depend not only on model parameters but also on the executable harnesses that organize information acquisition, tool use, reasoning, and memory. Recursive harness evolution can automate their design, but how it can reliably discover generalizable systems from limited, noisy, and repeatedly reused feedback remains unclear. We study this question in agentic video understanding, where rich multimodal evidence and coupled mechanisms make harness optimization challenging. We first introduce VideoHarness-BRSI, a recursive self-improvement framework that evolves video harnesses around a frozen vision-language model. Analyzing over 4,000 evolved candidates, we find it competitive with hand-engineered systems but identify three interconnected failure modes: validation overfitting, search-budget dilution, and the early-experience trap. We trace all three to a common root: the same imperfect validation evidence drives both harness revisions and the proposer’s search strategy. We therefore propose VideoHarness-SRSI, an evidence-governed framework in which every search decision rests on evidence that is transferable, sufficient, and gathered before the search commits. It restricts the proposer to content-free feedback, informs exploration with instrumented warm-up experiments, and develops mechanisms in isolated probe-race branches before factorial composition and joint refinement. Across four video understanding benchmarks, VideoHarness-SRSI improves the equally weighted mean held-out score by 6.2% relative to VideoHarness-BRSI and reduces the mean validation-test gap by 37.9%. Reliable harness evolution thus depends on how search is organized and informed, not only on how well it optimizes validation performance.

Details

Motivation: 胸片中的骨骼重叠会遮挡肺部发现；临床双能减影虽是基准方法，但受专用硬件和额外辐射限制。扩散方案还面临潜变量重建偏差、源—目标域差异及配对数据稀缺。

Result: DeBoneDiT 用预训练 Depth Anything V2 特征构造多层深度损失训练 Depth Auto-Encoder，以保留视觉保真和结构一致性；再将骨抑制建模为源条件 Brownian bridge 扩散，把原胸片同时作为扩散先验和结构条件。作者构建公开 SZCH-X-Rays，含 741 对胸片和 DES 软组织图。实验及下游评估称方法骨抑制效果优越且计算开销较低。摘要未报告数值指标或基线差距。

Insight: 深度特征监督可减轻潜空间压缩损失结构，Brownian bridge 条件则缩小胸片源图与软组织目标域的差异。数据规模为 741 对，摘要未给临床验证和辐射/硬件比较，不能量化实际诊断收益。

Abstract: Chest X-Ray (CXR) is a primary modality for diagnosing pulmonary diseases, yet the overlap of bony structures often obscures critical radiographic findings, increasing the risk of diagnostic uncertainty. While Dual-Energy Subtraction (DES) is the clinical benchmark for bone suppression, its widespread adoption is primarily hindered by specialized hardware requirements and increased radiation exposure. Recently, diffusion-based generative models have shown promise for this task, yet they are still constrained by reconstruction-biased latent compression, prior-induced domain gap, and a scarcity of high-quality paired datasets. To address these challenges, we propose DeBoneDiT, a depth-driven conditional bridge diffusion Transformer architecture for efficient high-resolution bone suppression. We first introduce Depth Auto-Encoder (DAE), a structure-aware vision tokenizer for latent compression. By incorporating a multi-level depth feature loss derived from the pretrained Depth Anything V2 encoder, DAE preserves both visual fidelity and structural consistency. Second, we formulate bone suppression as a source-conditioned Brownian bridge diffusion process, leveraging CXRs as both the diffusion prior and structural guidance to narrow the source-to-target domain gap and maintain anatomical plausibility. Furthermore, we have constructed and released SZCH-X-Rays, the largest publicly available high-quality paired dataset for bone suppression to date, comprising 741 pairs of CXR and DES soft tissue images. Extensive experiments and downstream evaluation demonstrate that DeBoneDiT achieves superior bone suppression performance with reduced computational overhead.

Abstract: Multimodal in-context learning (ICL) is an important capability of multimodal large language models (MLLMs). Its performance is closely tied to the demonstrations it uses, making demonstration retrieval a core problem for ICL. Common methods use fixed scoring methods to retrieve all the demonstrations at once, which may introduce redundancy and noise. Context-aware retrieval methods have emerged to address this problem by allowing incremental retrieval from a growing context. However, none of them lets the MLLM learn how to judge what new demonstrations it needs and then carry out progressive retrieval on its own. To this end, we introduce ActICL, which trains an MLLM agent to acquire and use an ICL context via tool-augmented reasoning. An ActICL agent analyzes its own information gaps during iterative reasoning and tool-use actions. Based on these gaps, it performs targeted retrieval to acquire a set of initial candidates. It can then use the existing context to further filter the candidates and refine the context, or it can iteratively perform a new retrieval. When the agent judges that the context is sufficient for the task, or when the resource limit is reached with the user request met, it sends the acquired context to an answering model, either the agent itself or another MLLM. We use a two-stage post-training procedure that jointly optimizes the agent’s acquisition and answering. With an adaptive number of demonstrations, ActICL improves average performance by 11.6 percentage points over standard similarity-based retrieval across seven benchmarks and three MLLMs, and by 4.4 points compared to the strongest prior baseline. The contexts acquired by ActICL agents can also be used effectively by frontier models. When GPT-5.6-Sol performs 4-shot ICL, ActICL’s contexts improve its performance by 3.5 points over standard retrieval, averaged over three benchmarks.

Abstract: Temporal action detection in long untrimmed videos suffers from inaccurate localization and low-quality proposals, especially with sparse annotations and ambiguous boundaries. We propose Hybrid Rank-Calibration of Action Proposals (HyrCap), a proposal-centric query-based TAD framework for temporal event understanding. Hybrid combines complementary video features with different localization cues, while Rank-Calibration adjusts proposal ranking confidence to reflect localization reliability and candidate competition. HyrCap treats dense query-based predictions as an over-complete temporal event hypothesis space. Abstract: Text-to-image models increasingly generate readable Chinese text that still contains local rendering defects, such as a character with an extra or missing stroke or a malformed component. Such errors are invisible to OCR and vision-language models, which are trained for recognition and are therefore invariant to the very structural deviations that define a rendering error. We formalize text rendering verification as a target-conditioned problem: given a target character and an image crop, decide whether the crop is a correct rendering of that character. Our verifier TEXTVERIFIER feeds the text tower a character’s Ideographic Description Sequence (IDS) rather than a single-character symbol, and is trained on 12.7M crop-IDS pairs plus label-free “same-character, different-quality” hard pairs sampled from the training trajectory of a text-region inpainting model. TEXTVERIFIER reaches an F1 of 0.844, far above OCR confidence (0.526), a general VLM (0.329), and TextPecker (0.590). Used as a per-character reward for Flow-GRPO post-training, it further reduces rendering errors, showing that its score can both detect and correct errors.

Abstract: Lipreading remains challenging due to the inherent ambiguity of visual speech cues and their sensitivity to head pose and video resolution. In this work, we propose FLARE, a framework for robust visual speech representation learning via cross-modal distillation and joint face-lip modeling. FLARE leverages semantically rich Whisper representations as the acoustic teacher signal, converting them into discrete distillation targets through a random-projection quantizer and pretraining the model with a masked prediction objective. To capture complementary visual information, FLARE employs a dual-stream architecture that jointly processes lip region-of-interest (ROI) and full-face inputs, where the lip stream captures finegrained articulatory motion and the face stream encodes broader facial dynamics. During pretraining, auxiliary audio input is incorporated to facilitate cross-modal alignment, and modality dropout is applied to encourage robust visual-only representations. For downstream lipreading, FLARE serves as a visual encoder coupled with either a lightweight Transformer decoder or an LLM-based decoder. Evaluated on LRS2, LRS3, and the out-of-domain WildVSR benchmark, FLARE achieves strong lipreading performance, with word error rates of 11.7%, 13.2%, and 34.3%, respectively. A progressive design study confirms the contribution of each major component. Further analyses demonstrate that joint face-lip modeling improves robustness under degraded video resolution and large head pose variation, while yielding more phoneme-discriminative visual representations. Code and pretrained models are available at https://github.com/jxzhanggg/FLARE.

Abstract: Safety alignment of text-to-image diffusion models aims to suppress harmful content without compromising useful generation capabilities. However, preferences over completed images compress multidimensional generation factors into a coarse signal, potentially driving changes beyond those required for safety. To address this, we propose Safety Transition Preference Optimization (SaTPO), which enforces step-wise alignment during denoising. SaTPO leverages the insight that an unsafe prompt and its safe rewrite naturally form a tightly coupled preference pair when conditioned on a shared parent state and noise realization. By anchoring optimization on identical parent states, SaTPO eliminates historical trajectory divergence, confining safety adaptation to an incremental alignment while preserving task-relevant semantics. Multi-scale drift constraints and benign replay further limit behavioral deviation and preserve ordinary generation. We additionally construct CoProPV, a refined dataset ensuring visually verified contrasts. Experiments on Stable Diffusion v1.5 and SDXL demonstrate improved safety-quality trade-offs. Compared with AlignGuard, PerspectiveVision unsafe-detection rates on Inappropriate Image Prompts decrease from 12.70% to 6.00% and from 15.04% to 9.30%, respectively, while FID improves from 32.28 to 25.95 and from 36.50 to 27.51. Motivation: 多图像视觉语言模型的总体准确率难以揭示推理流水线中的失败环节；多图像任务既要求在序列中找出目标实例、识别外观，也要求应用专属视觉码本并按序组合名称。

Details

Motivation: 逐步生成的每个采样步直觉上似乎可对应一个视觉推理步骤，但作者指出，仅增加普通流匹配采样步数未必增加模型的序贯表达能力，因此需要解释迭代生成如何真正支持更深推理。

Result: 理论上，在模型输出满足若干温和正则条件时，普通流匹配采样轨迹可由常数轮并行模型评估模拟；在标准Transformer复杂度模型下，增加采样步数因而不能提升常数深度骨干的表达能力。作者进一步指出，自条件将前一步对最终图像的估计作为后续条件，能够移除此瓶颈。多种视觉推理任务上的受控实验显示，自条件流匹配比普通流匹配准确率更高、推理深度更大，且性能随采样步数增加；理论结果还表明，采用自条件时常数深度骨干可达到与空间有界图灵机等价的表达能力。

Insight: 关键设计启示是让后续采样步骤继承此前计算形成的信息；单纯增加迭代次数不足以保证深度推理。该结论依赖论文陈述的正则条件和复杂度设定，摘要未给出各任务的定量增益。

Abstract: Recent work has explored visual reasoning through image generation, where models solve visual-reasoning problems by directly generating solution images. Intuitively, iterative samplers such as flow matching are well suited to this task, since each sampling step may resemble one reasoning step. However, in this work, from a circuit complexity perspective, we show that this intuition is misleading. We prove that as long as the model outputs satisfy certain mild regularity conditions, the trajectory of the vanilla flow-matching sampler can be simulated by a constant number of sequential rounds of parallel model evaluations. Consequently, additional sampling steps cannot increase the expressive power of a constant-depth backbone model under the standard complexity-theoretic model of transformers. We then reveal that self-conditioning, which conditions the model on the previous step’s estimate of the final image, plays a previously unrecognized role in removing this bottleneck. Controlled experiments on diverse visual-reasoning tasks show that flow matching with self-conditioning achieves higher accuracy and greater reasoning depth than vanilla flow matching, and its performance improves with more sampling steps. Theoretically, we prove that with self-conditioning, a constant-depth backbone can achieve expressive power equivalent to that of space-bounded Turing machines. Our study suggests a design principle for future visual reasoning research: carry more information between sampling steps, so that additional steps translate into deeper reasoning.

Abstract: Geometry foundation models estimate camera poses and 3D scene structure from video sequences, but maintaining consistent reconstructions over long horizons remains challenging. Feed-forward models process long sequences as independent, fixed-length frame windows and align their predictions, causing pose and reconstruction errors to accumulate across windows. Streaming models retain information from past frames to inform future predictions, but cannot revise earlier estimates when later observations reveal errors. To address this, we introduce Scaffold MemOry for Revision (SMORE), a framework equipping frozen geometry foundation models with a revisable external memory for long-horizon inference. Inspired by hippocampal associative memory in biological brains, which links spatial context with sensory experience, SMORE associates where an observer is with what it observes, enabling past observations to be stored, retrieved, verified against new evidence, and revised when inconsistencies are detected. We evaluate SMORE with eight competitive geometry models on five datasets spanning camera pose estimation, multi-view depth estimation, 3D point-cloud reconstruction, simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM), and novel view synthesis. Across all evaluated models, SMORE consistently improves long-sequence pose estimation, reduces multi-view depth error by up to 58%, reduces Chamfer distance for 3D point-cloud reconstruction by up to 43%, and achieves average trajectory error as low as 2.9 cm in long-horizon SLAM. By retaining corrected poses and observations, SMORE further improves the fidelity and spatial consistency of large-scale 3D scene reconstruction. These results establish revisable external memory as a flexible interface for extending pretrained geometry models beyond fixed-context prediction toward iterative, error-correcting inference over long visual sequences.

Result: SceneAE用显式三维坐标及隐式内容特征表示局部场景区域，形成稀疏、无结构的潜在点云。用于重建时，生成后的持久三维表示可投影到不同目标相机并解码成RGB-D观测，无需针对新的相机轨迹重新生成视频。用于生成时，先利用几何与内容分离生成受相机控制的三维几何，再以几何为条件合成内容，以减轻相机与外观的纠缠。摘要报告实验在生成质量、计算成本和相机可控性方面均取得更优结果。

Insight: 把潜变量锚定到场景本身而非某个视点，能统一支持跨相机重建和分阶段生成，并减少相机—外观耦合。所给摘要正文在方法描述中途截断，且未给出数据集、指标、基线或数值，因而只能报告其定性结论。

Abstract: Recent video world models have increasingly adopted 3D memory mechanisms to integrate historical observations into a lightweight and persistent scene representation. This naturally raises a complementary question: if 3D representations can serve as an effective mechanism for memorizing observed scenes, can they also be directly used as the latent space for reconstructing and generating scenes? Motivated by this idea, we represent a scene as a compact and unstructured 3D-anchored latent space (\textbf{SceneAE}). Each latent combines an explicit 3D position with an implicit content feature and is spatially grounded to describe its local scene region, forming a sparse latent point cloud. Unlike conventional video-VAE latents that describe how a scene appears from particular viewpoints, our representation directly describes the scene itself, making it naturally applicable to both reconstruction and generation. For reconstruction, once the scene has been generated, our persistent 3D representation can be projected and decoded into RGB-D observations by different target cameras, without the need to regenerate a video for each new camera trajectory. For generation, we leverage the geometry-content separation to first generate camera-controlled 3D geometry and then synthesize content conditioned on it, reducing camera-appearance entanglement and improving camera controllability. Experiments show that this 3D latent achieves superior generation quality, computational cost, and camera controllability.

Abstract: Monocular world-space human recovery methods excel on current benchmarks, but these rarely include fast camera motion. We introduce the K-Pop stress test, broadcast dance videos that combine fast, large camera motion with dense multi-person choreography. Leading methods produce plausible per-frame meshes on these videos, yet their world-space trajectories drift far from the performers’ stage positions. Through a systematic diagnosis, we show that the camera and human estimates each remain self-consistent, and that the drift appears when they are combined at mismatched scales. Each is recovered at its own scale, so a mismatch between the two can turn camera motion into human drift that grows with camera displacement and becomes severe in broadcast dance videos. We derive a closed-form correction that estimates this mismatch and applies one scale to all performers, removing the drift while keeping their relative positions in one shared world frame. It requires no retraining and works with existing backends. Combined with physical constraints, the correction yields stable world-space reconstruction for choreography modeling, scene editing, data generation, and virtual reshooting.

Abstract: Video large language models (Video LLMs) often generate responses unsupported by the input video. Contrastive decoding (CD) can mitigate these hallucinations, but its effectiveness depends on how the negative branch weakens visual evidence. Existing methods rely on predefined perturbations or masking guided by question reconstruction, offering limited control over the model’s visual reliance during answer generation. We introduce Attention-guided Representation Perturbation for Contrastive Decoding (ARP-CD), a training-free framework for constructing contrastive negatives directly in representation space. ARP-CD uses the attention mass assigned to visual tokens at the answer-prediction position to guide bounded gradient updates of the language model’s input visual hidden states. These updates reduce visual attention and produce a negative input, whose predictions are contrasted with those of the original branch throughout generation. Experiments across four Video LLMs and four benchmarks show that ARP-CD outperforms representative training-free baselines in most model–benchmark settings, reducing hallucinations while improving general video understanding. Further analyses show that attention-guided perturbation directions improve negative quality and that the resulting negatives provide effective contrastive references for correcting the original predictions.

Abstract: Crash Moment Localization aims to localize the major physical impact that interrupts controlled flight in an onboard first-person-view (FPV) video recorded by a drone, thereby providing a temporal reference for retrospective crash analysis in aerial robotics. However, existing crash-video datasets predominantly target surveillance or dash-camera footage, and their annotation schemes and task formulations are not designed for temporal localization of the major physical impact in UAV onboard FPV videos. To address this gap, we present FPV-CRASH, a dataset and fixed-protocol reference evaluation for offline Crash Moment Localization in drone FPV videos. FPV-CRASH contains 1,052 drone FPV clips (526 Crash and 526 Normal); each Crash clip has a single-frame reference annotation for the Crash Moment, and every clip is paired with four English captions automatically generated from sampled frames and the known Crash/Normal label. Abstract: Video world models aim to simulate complex environments with realistic appearance and dynamics. Remembering past observations alone may not be sufficient in every scenario, as they may no longer reflect the world after a sequence of actions. We introduce a paradigm that decouples a persistent, updatable abstract state from the visual state. We propose StateCraft, a novel architecture that pairs a language model with a video diffusion model to evolve the dynamic world. Abstract: Text-to-video (T2V) generation models can produce visually realistic videos that nevertheless violate the physical consequences implied by the prompt and scene. Existing benchmarks largely rely on holistic plausibility scores or broad quality dimensions, leaving it unclear which physical behavior is being tested and why a model fails. We introduce PhyMetric, a scene-level diagnostic benchmark that recasts physical plausibility evaluation as verifiable, scene-grounded question answering. PhyMetric contains 1{,}115 scenes spanning 50 physical categories across six domains, paired with 8{,}920 answer-balanced binary questions over 12 evaluation dimensions. Each scene is associated with eight targeted yes/no questions and a structured physical prior specifying the governing law, expected temporal sequence, observable signatures, and common failure modes. At evaluation time, a vision-language model (VLM) judge answers each question from question-aware sampled frames conditioned on the prior, and correctness is hierarchically aggregated into scene, dimension, physical category, and model scores. Experiments on four open-source T2V models and three VLM judges show that scene-specific binary QA is a critical component of the protocol: when each targeted question is replaced by a single generic plausibility query, evaluator accuracy drops to the chance level. Structured physical priors offer a complementary signal that improves human alignment of the resulting scores, with effects that vary across VLM judges of different capacity. These findings establish PhyMetric as a benchmark that turns physical plausibility evaluation from opaque holistic scoring into interpretable, scene-level inspection. Result: 作者发现，视频编解码器的组图像量化模式保留类别区分性动态，运动矢量和残差变化则在动作转变附近呈现明显响应。CoDy将编码器信号构成的视图与RGB、光流融合，以置信度感知共识聚类生成软伪标签；再将编解码动态转为类别无关的边界先验，监督视觉边界学习并指导候选初始化和迭代细化。编解码器分支只用于训练，推理时无额外编解码侧计算。在THUMOS14和ActivityNet v1.3上，作者报告CoDy持续优于现有无监督方法并达到最先进定位表现。

Insight: 视频压缩过程中的编码信号包含可供动作发现和边界定位使用的无标注动态信息，可补充视觉特征。摘要未列出各指标的具体数值或提升幅度，且编码器分支只在训练阶段参与。

Abstract: Unsupervised temporal action localization (UTAL) aims to identify action categories and localize their temporal boundaries without annotation supervision. Existing methods typically derive supervision for both tasks solely from RGB and optical-flow representations, often resulting in noisy category assignments and inaccurate temporal boundaries. In this work, we reveal an overlooked property of video codecs: group-of-pictures quantization patterns preserve category-discriminative dynamics, while motion-vector and residual variations exhibit pronounced responses around action transitions. Based on this insight, we propose CoDy, a codec dynamics-guided framework that exploits the codec as complementary information to the visual view for UTAL. CoDy integrates a codec-enhanced view with RGB and flow through confidence-aware consensus clustering, producing reliable soft pseudo-labels. It further converts codec dynamics into a class-agnostic boundary prior, which supervises visual boundary learning and guides proposal initialization and iterative refinement. The codec branches are only used during the training stage and introduce no extra codec-side computation at inference. Extensive experiments on THUMOS14 and ActivityNet v1.3 demonstrate that CoDy consistently outperforms existing UTAL methods and achieves state-of-the-art localization performance. These results establish codec signals as effective annotation-free priors for jointly inferring which actions occur and when they occur.

Abstract: Instruction-driven image editing has reached native 4K, yet how such editors are trained remains undisclosed, and pretraining one from scratch is beyond most teams. The task itself compounds the difficulty: a single end-to-end editor cannot make precise parametric adjustments, match specialized experts, or interpret the vague instructions users actually write. We argue that 4K editing is a system problem rather than a single-model problem, and present BluePixel, which couples a native-4K editing model with an agent harness; the complete training recipe is disclosed in this paper. The model makes 4K training practical: it turns a 1K-pretrained editor into a native-4K one through progressive VAE adaptation that keeps 4K training as affordable as 1K, absorbs a long tail of vertical editing skills through continual learning, and refines the aesthetic-fidelity trade-off with reinforcement learning. Distillation via source-anchored target projection (SATP) then compresses 50 denoising steps to 4, completing a 4K edit in 8.7 s on a single Kunlunxin P800 XPU while preserving task success rate and background consistency. The harness, BluePixel Agent, makes editing controllable: it decomposes user instructions into specialist and general operations, verifies every result with the same reward model that guided training, and retries within bounded attempts. Abstract: Segment Anything Model 3 (SAM 3) is a foundation model for image and video segmentation that sets the state of the art in visual object tracking. Its tracker, inherited from SAM 2, stores information from past frames in a memory bank, managed by a simple First-In-First-Out rule, allowing temporal consistency in complex videos. Several recent methods improve SAM 2 with hand-crafted memory update rules, each targeting a specific failure mode, such as distractors, occlusions, or object motion. However, their gains transfer only partially to the stronger SAM 3 backbone. We instead present a more general, data-driven approach that frames memory control as a sequential decision-making task. SAM3RL is an extension of the SAM 3 tracker with a lightweight memory controller, trained by reinforcement learning, that decides which frames enter the memory bank in order to maximize tracking performance. The same approach applied to SAM 2 gives SAM2RL, which outperforms hand-crafted rules on 9 of the 12 benchmarks, showing the generality of our method across model generations.

Motivation: 冰下AUV视频的三维重建精度不佳，但此前不清楚问题主要来自重建算法，还是采集系统本身的局限。

[49] GeoCloak: An Agentic Framework for Semantics-Preserving Geolocation Privacy via Peripheral Clue Manipulation cs.CV | cs.AI | cs.CRPDF

Yixin Wu, Mingjie Li, Yun Shen, Michael Backes, Yang Zhang

TL;DR: GeoCloak利用地理定位线索常位于图像外围但对地点推断影响大的不对称性，结合定向语义编辑、对抗扰动和效用反馈，降低视觉语言模型从照片推断城市的准确率。

Result: GeoCloak先分析图像以识别与地理位置相关的线索，再规划定向语义编辑以抑制外围线索，并自主配置对抗扰动削弱剩余地理特征；整个过程由迭代的效用反馈约束。作者在覆盖108个国家和576座城市的四个数据集上，针对五种大型视觉语言模型和三种推断方法评估。城市级总体准确率从41.2%降至9.9%，约为最强基线残余准确率的一半，同时在定性和定量图像效用保持方面显著优于基线。

Insight: 隐私防护可优先针对决定地理位置、但视觉上处于外围的语义线索，再处理剩余表征，以减少对整体图像语义的破坏。结果限于摘要所列数据集、模型及推断方法；摘要未给出各类效用指标明细及真实用户场景验证。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of large vision language models (LVLMs) has made geolocation inference from images highly accurate and accessible. This poses serious privacy risks: a passive adversary with black-box LVLM access can infer a user’s location from a single photo with no metadata. Existing protection methods rely almost exclusively on adversarial perturbation, which introduces perceptible visual artifacts and utility degradation. We identify the visual-geolocation asymmetry: geolocation-decisive clues, such as street signs, tend to be visually peripheral but dominate location inference. Exploiting this asymmetry, we propose GeoCloak, an agentic framework for semantics-preserving geolocation privacy protection. It acts as a clue-aware planner that analyzes each image to identify geolocation-relevant clues, dispatches targeted semantic edits to directly suppress peripheral clues, and autonomously configures adversarial perturbation to further suppress residual geolocation features, all under iterative utility-aware feedback. We evaluate GeoCloak on four diverse datasets spanning 108 countries and 576 cities against five LVLMs and three inference methods. GeoCloak reduces overall city-level accuracy from 41.2% to 9.9%, approximately half of the best-performing baseline, while achieving substantially superior utility preservation both qualitatively and quantitatively. We hope this work takes a meaningful step toward practical, semantics-preserving geolocation privacy protection, giving users greater control over the location information inadvertently embedded in their shared images.

First, their category coverage is narrow: most benchmarks span only a small number of categories across a few domains, falling far short of the open-ended scale of real-world visual entities. Second, their task construction is often insufficiently rigorous: models can answer correctly using coarse-grained cues, vague associations, or elimination strategies without truly mastering precise concepts, making it difficult to accurately assess their knowledge of fine-grained entities. We introduce VisualWorldBench, a taxonomy-grounded multi-task benchmark built on a hierarchical taxonomy of 21,291 leaf-node classes across 8 visual domains, with 32,541 evaluation instances whose ground-truth labels cover 15,479 categories across four complementary tasks: closed-set recognition, contrastive selection, precise localization, and open-world naming, covering different directions of knowledge retrieval and varying degrees of answer openness to comprehensively evaluate visual world knowledge. Most questions are constructed from taxonomically proximate categories, forcing precise discrimination among fine-grained entities. Experiments reveal that the strongest closed-source model achieves only 80.4% average accuracy, exposing capability gaps hidden under conventional benchmarks. We further construct VisualWorldBench-50K, a training resource covering 50K+ categories. Fine-tuning on VisualWorldBench-50K yields over 21 percentage points improvement on VisualWorldBench, with consistent transfer to public fine-grained recognition and visual knowledge benchmarks.

[52] When Pairwise Preferences Cycle: A Reliability-Anchored Preference Walk for Video GRPO cs.CVPDF

Xinrui Lin, Fan Li, Shumin Wang, Dehong Kong, Yanyong Zhang

TL;DR: 该研究审计视频生成GRPO组内的成对偏好图，发现循环偏好普遍存在且持续至训练中，并提出可靠性锚定偏好游走以结合胜率与图关系证据来计算优势。

Details

Motivation: 成对比较通常比单点标量评分更适合评价生成视频，但若只按平均胜率聚合，偏好图中的关系结构会被丢弃，可能使个别rollout的优势估计不可靠并影响强化学习优化。

Result: 作者把每个GRPO组的样本构造成偏好图，节点对应采样rollout、边记录评估器的成对偏好；审计6,976个在线比较图后发现循环偏好十分常见，且贯穿训练过程。针对仅平均胜率的聚合方法，作者提出Reliability-Anchored Preference Walk，以可靠性引导的图游走将全局胜率信号与循环偏好图中的可靠关系证据结合起来计算优势。在评估器、比较协议、组规模、提示调度和查询预算匹配的条件下，该方法无需在每组已观察比较之外额外查询评估器，并在受控比较、迁移设置和盲道人评中优于Pairwise-GRPO。

Insight: 循环成对偏好包含可利用的关系信息，不应在优势估计时简单压成彼此独立的平均胜率；该方法在固定查询预算下重新利用已采集比较。摘要没有给出具体提升幅度、循环偏好率或人评设置细节。

Abstract: Pairwise preference feedback has become a widely adopted supervision signal for reinforcement learning in video generation models, as pairwise comparisons provide a more reliable alternative than pointwise scalar ratings for evaluating the quality of generated videos. Specifically, we define a preference graph for each GRPO group under a pairwise evaluator, where nodes represent sampled rollouts and edges encode pairwise preferences. Auditing 6,976 on-policy comparison graphs, we find that cyclic preferences are prevalent and persist throughout training. Naive pairwise reward aggregation based on average win rates discards the underlying comparison structures and treats each rollout independently, which may introduce unreliable advantage estimates for certain rollouts and consequently affect reinforcement learning optimization. Therefore, we propose the \emph{Reliability-Anchored Preference Walk} (\ours{}), a graph-aware advantage aggregation method that strengthens the global preference signal from win rates with reliable relational evidence from cyclic preference graphs through a reliability-guided preference walk. Under matched evaluator, comparison protocol, group size, prompt schedule, and query budget, \ours{} requires no additional evaluator queries beyond the observed comparisons within each rollout group and improves over Pairwise-GRPO across controlled comparisons, transfer settings, and blind human evaluations.

Details

Motivation: 视频场景图生成要同时利用对象身份持续性和关系突变。已有跨帧聚合方法侧重一致性，可能掩盖关系转变，使模型在关系已经变化后仍保留上一帧预测。

Result: 作者提出单阶段关系转变感知Transformer（RTAformer），分别建模实体持续性和关系变化；通过注入相邻帧关系查询之间经校准的差异来引导谓词更新，并在训练中进行跨帧实例对齐以减少表征漂移。在Action Genome上的实验显示，相较既有方法，召回率和平均召回率均有所提高。消融支持各组成模块的贡献；使用匹配骨干的比较还显示，在关系转变帧上的增益大于非转变帧。

Abstract: Video scene graph generation (VidSGG) aims to capture objects and their evolving relationships in videos. This requires accounting for both the persistence of object identities and abrupt changes in relationships. Existing methods aggregate information across frames to exploit temporal context, but an emphasis on consistency can obscure these changes. As a result, a model may retain its previous prediction even after the underlying relation has changed. We refer to this failure mode as relational semantic inertia. To address this issue, we propose Relation-Transition-Aware Transformer (RTAformer), a one-stage framework that separately models entity persistence and relation changes. It injects calibrated differences between adjacent-frame relation queries to guide predicate updates, while cross-frame instance alignment during training helps reduce cross-frame representational drift. Experiments on Action Genome show improvements in recall and mean recall over existing methods, with ablation studies supporting the contributions of the proposed components and comparisons using matched backbones showing larger gains on relation-transition frames than on non-transition frames.

Abstract: Distilling pretrained video diffusion models into few-step autoregressive (AR) generators typically combines student initialization with distribution matching distillation (DMD). An intermediate AR teacher can provide causally compatible targets for ODE initialization. However, causally compatible targets alone do not ensure high-quality generation under a short sampling schedule. We analyze the posterior-mean predictions favored by direct latent regression and empirically find that causal ODE initialization alleviates blur but still leaves fine detail unresolved. To address this limitation, we propose Representation Forcing, which learns few-step causal initialization through representation-space supervision rather than intermediate-teacher trajectory regression. The student is trained with teacher forcing by comparing clean predictions and training targets through normalized features of a frozen video diffusion transformer. This phase reuses the synthetic videos generated by a bidirectional model for AR teacher training and replaces both teacher training and causal ODE distillation, without generating additional causal ODE trajectories. The subsequent asymmetric DMD procedure remains unchanged. Empirical results show that, at four sampling steps, RepForcing better preserves generation quality than intermediate-teacher-based initialization before DMD. After DMD, our four-step chunk-wise model improves VBench Total from 84.04 to 84.66 (+0.62 points) and VisionReward from 6.326 to 10.074 (+59.25%) over the reported causal-ODE-based baseline.

Abstract: Human mesh recovery (HMR) provides a geometric foundation for understanding human motion, but accurately reconstructing both body motion and fine hand articulation from videos remains challenging, especially in real-world scenes where occlusion and partial visibility are common. Video generation pretraining offers rich spatial and temporal priors for this task, but its focus on visual synthesis leaves a gap between these representations and explicit human geometry. We present DreamHMR, a general framework that adapts video generation priors for complete body-and-hand reconstruction. It follows a global-to-local strategy, first grounding video representations in human geometry and then refining body and hand detail with reliable local evidence. Global Body Representation Transfer trains the video backbone to predict complete human renderings, turning its native prediction task into a means of learning surface correspondence and posed geometry. Local Joint Feature Enhancement combines contextual estimates with reliable local evidence to recover fine body and hand detail, while limiting interference from occluders and background. Abstract: Current video object removal methods suffer from prohibitive latency (minutes per video) and non-causal processing that prevents real-time interaction. We present StreamErase, enabling sub-second latency with up to 26 FPS streaming generation on a single GPU. Our approach first augments a text-to-video model with mask input to generate high-quality videos that erase target objects and synthesize coherent backgrounds, but does not perform inference on the fly. As such, we progressively transfer the erasing capability from a powerful bidirectional foundation model to an efficient unidirectional streaming eraser, enabling long-horizon erasing without sacrificing visual fidelity. Several key challenges arise when generating videos over long, potentially infinite horizons: (1) bridging the domain gap from training on finite length and extrapolating to long horizons, (2) sustaining high quality by preventing error accumulation, and (3) maintaining fast inference, without increasing computational cost as the context grows. To address these challenges, we carefully designed sliding-window causal attention with attention sinks. By incorporating self-rollout with attention sinks and KV cache rolling during training, we simulate inference-time extrapolations with a fixed context window, enabling constant-speed generation of arbitrarily long videos. Finally, we establish a dedicated benchmark, LongErase, for streaming long-video object removal. Our model achieves strong performance compared to existing video object erasing models and generalizes well to real-world video scenarios, uniquely enabling infinite-length streaming. With StreamErase, users can input a mask and see the erasing results unfold in real-time, delivering a truly interactive experience.

Result: Glance Forcing冻结预训练教师，将去噪轨迹解耦为互补的慢、快专家，并为不同阶段配置目标。作者还指出，PF-ODE蒸馏主要使少步轨迹几何变直，而后续分布匹配阶段单独负责分布质量。只用一条PF-ODE轨迹时，该方法VBench Total从充分训练的SOTA基线84.04升至84.22，同时把轨迹采集成本从数千GPU小时降到约一张A100 GPU小时；动态程度、VisionReward及指令跟随的增益更大，人类评审也持续偏好该方法。

Insight: 只减少轨迹数量会造成质量崩塌，但按噪声阶段分解优化可显著降低监督采集成本；轨迹几何匹配与生成分布匹配承担不同作用。约一A100 GPU小时是摘要所报轨迹采集成本，不等同于全部训练和推理成本；具体增幅及人评规模未给出。

Abstract: Autoregressive generation underlies real-time, causal video synthesis and is the backbone of video world models. Today’s dominant approach distills a bidirectional diffusion model into a causal few-step student, but existing pipelines make this conversion prohibitively expensive, requiring offline Probability Flow ODE} (PF-ODE) trajectories that cost tens of thousands of GPU-hours and terabytes of storage. We show it can be made dramatically more efficient. Pushing this efficiency to its extreme, we reduce trajectory supervision to a single sample, the strictest setting possible, and find that it causes severe quality collapse. We trace this to the teacher’s trajectory geometry: the high-noise regime, governing semantic layout and motion, and the low-noise regime, governing fine texture exhibit fundamentally different curvature and optimization behavior, so a single monolithic student cannot jointly fit both, causing severe representation interference. Motivated by this, we propose Glance Forcing, a lightweight distillation framework that decouples the denoising trajectory into two complementary slow and fast experts, each guided by a tailored, stage-specific objective, while keeping the pretrained teacher entirely frozen. This decoupling further reveals a general mechanism underlying few-step distillation: PF-ODE distillation mainly straightens the few-step trajectory geometry, while the subsequent distribution-matching stage independently governs distributional quality. Using only one PF-ODE trajectory, Glance Forcing already surpasses the fully-trained state-of-the-art baseline, raising the VBench Total score from 84.04 to 84.22 while cutting trajectory-collection cost from thousands of GPU-hours to about one A100-GPU-hour, with markedly larger gains in dynamic degree, VisionReward, and instruction-following, and consistent preference from human raters over strong baselines.

Abstract: A multiplayer world model must predict how the independent actions of several players change a shared environment, including changes outside a player’s current field of view. Learning this task therefore requires synchronized POV and panoramic videos of interacting players together with their actions, yet no existing dataset provides this combination in Minecraft, an established testbed in which players act independently in a shared, modifiable 3D world. We present PAWA, a dataset and framework for action-conditioned panoramic multiplayer world modeling. The dataset pairs each player’s POV video with world-stabilized panoramic video and aligned actions. Its distributed pipeline decouples CPU gameplay capture from GPU rendering, scales beyond 64 simultaneous players, and re-renders each recorded session under new observation configurations without replaying the gameplay. Abstract: Large view synthesis models (LVSMs) reconstruct scenes from sparse views and render novel views quickly, but they incur unnecessary storage and rendering costs due to token redundancy. We present GLoD-LVSM, a compact, geometry-grounded scene representation for LVSMs with continuous levels of detail. First, instead of learning a fixed set of scene anchor initializations, GLoD-LVSM learns a continuous field that initializes a flexible number of anchors to match the desired budget. A condenser then aggregates scene information into these anchors, with both the field and condenser trained end-to-end. Second, the condenser infers reference-token and anchor geometry to guide compression through geometry-grounded attention, improving rendering quality and enabling frustum culling during decoding. Compared with existing LVSM scene-compression baselines, GLoD-LVSM achieves the best trade-off between rendering quality and scene token count. Compared with the uncompressed base model, GLoD-LVSM reduces the scene-token count by 33.4% and accelerates rendering by $1.23\times$ with no quality loss. Under a relaxed quality-loss budget, the reduction increases to 85.5%, with $3.00\times$ rendering speedup. Beyond rendering, we further show that our scene representation also supports scene-semantic understanding, highlighting its potential for broader downstream tasks.

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly deployed in safety-critical domains, yet their susceptibility to backdoor attacks remains a formidable challenge. While existing defenses have evolved from visual-centric patch cues to unsupervised internal attention statistics, they are fundamentally constrained by a reliance on static snapshots and attack-specific heuristics. Given the intricate interplay between dual modalities, the vast design space for multimodal triggers allows diverse threats, such as textual injections, sub-perceptual noises, and coordinated cross-modal triggers, to easily circumvent such rigid priors, particularly when attack types are unknown in real-world applications. In this paper, we rethink backdoor detection by shifting the diagnostic paradigm from static observations to dynamic output evolution. We observe that a successful attack inevitably hijacks the model’s decoding trajectory, skewing internal modal contributions during generation while maintaining an abnormal output invariance despite input perturbations. To capture this behavior, we propose EVO-Detect, a unified detection framework that monitors the dynamic evolution of internal signals during early decoding steps through two complementary channels: Attention Flow and Logits Flow. The attention branch measures how image-text attention allocation evolves during early generation, whereas the logits branch quantifies the sensitivity of the output distribution to modal perturbations through interventional ablations. We then convert these trajectories into compact sample-level metrics and normalize them against negative-control signatures so that natural perturbations are less likely to be mistaken for backdoor evidence. Extensive evaluations show that our approach substantially outperforms state-of-the-art baselines such as BYE and TCAP across diverse attack families.
